8a5('a): gsum/gru gsum mthing nag dbus na bri//zla kham gsum la phrag thod gsum/khrag dang dug dang ming dang rus//kha bskor dpon g.yog sngags kyang bri/zla kham drug la sgo nga drug/sgyid drug sngags drug phur pa drug/zla kham phyir ltas/

8b4('a): thun drug phur drag sngags drug dgod//drag sngags dgug sngags zla kham sngogs/rim pa bzhin du bri bar bya//mtshe ljang snying sna'i sngags kyang bri//pho thod mo thod steng dang 'og/ya dang ma ni bris pa'i//zur gsum dag la re re ma nan/tshe dang
8b5('a): 'brang rgyas mchod pa dang /sngags kyis bsgrub pa'i maN+Dal yin//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba/gru gsum mtha' ras gsum ldan lig/log la phyi bltas zla kham gsum//zur du nang ltas zla kham gsum/dbusu rtsa ba'i maN+Dal bri//tshe dang

8'a1('a): @dgu dgod//kha dog dmar nag dag tu bya//zur du thod pa gsum po gnas/dbu su rtsa ba'i snying po bri/khyam+ba ni nag po dag tu btang /dug thun khal thun la sogs bca'//thun gyi maN+Dal bya ba yin//de nas yang dag

8'a7('a): chasi dag tu zhugs/g.ya su mdung thogs g.yon du thags//phyar nag lus la gyon nas ni//thun khrag lus la dkon nas ni//'phags pa rdo rje nag mo dbang phyug che gi ma'i sngags rgyud las//phrin las bsgrub pa'i le'u ste bzhi/
8'b1('a): pa'o//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba/dpa ldan nag mo chen mo'i//smod pa'i na yang rtsi bar bya//byi rlabs byed pa'i sngags mtshan dgu/me'i sa bon yu yis brgyad/sna grangs bdun du bshad pa te/lha'i snying po es
8'b2('a): rgyan nas//smod pa bcu bzhi'i cig dgod/nga yis sna drangs bcug ste/de la dbus brgyan bcu gcig pa'o//gongs blangs sna drangs dgu pa'o//na ni bdun du 'dod pa yin no//ka la ya rtag is spros pa/dgu'i rnam pa tshang ba'o//ma yis sna
8'b3('a): drangs dgur 'dod pas//tsa la es sgras bco brgyad//dang po ru ru rag mo zhes//tha ma ma ra ya zhes pa//thag bzang ris la smos pa yin//U U o zhes bya bas spras//e ra tsa ma ra ya zhes pa//lha'i gzhonu rnams la smos/
8'b4('a): e e gnyis kyis mdzes pa'i sgra//da ni dun ri du la ya/badz+ra ma ra ya zhes pa/rgyal chen bzhi'i 'khor la smos/e u e u rnam pas mtshan//du ru tshA ni da ha na badz+ra ma ra ya/nA ge ba shu lha ma yin/smod pa u A i spris spras/
8'b5('a): du ma ra ha badz+ra b+h+yo//ma ra ya zhes bya bas ni//phag gi sna can smod pa ste/u o ha ha a yas kyang mtshan//na ga ra ga zhes pa ni//ma ra ya zhes kun la spras/klu'i sgra ste bram ze'i rigs//kyi ma srog dad kyi ma me ma ra ya ste sod cig/
8'b6('a): sgrang rgyal rig klu la smos pa yin//ka la yas rgyan i yang ka/sa la mos mdzes o dang ldan//gong ma bzhin du rgyan kyang de'o//sa dang rtsa bsde bas gsod pa'i sgra/ma ra tig dang me ra tig/gsod pa'i sgra de ma ra yA/rje'u rig dmod pa i I
8'b7('a): brgyan tse tsen dang ma ri tig/gong ma lta bur brjod pa dang /ma ra yang yang shi bur gradags/rig dar klu'i dmod pa yin//e i i e e is/spras pas rang byung ldan pa'o//byin gyis rlobs pa'i ngo mod pa dgu/rnam shes brgyad kyi
9a1('a): gshed ma ste/ral gri lta bu dmod pa la/ngar blung nga mo sngags byin rlob pa'o//de la dmod pa'i thang pa ni/na yang grangasu brtsi ba ni//dang ldan dpon g.yogs rmod pa la/de la bcu'i drug dag dang //bdun drug ldan pa'i mtshon cha can
9a2('a): thang mu le thang mu le/u ed u e bzhi dang ldan/rtsa thang rtsa thang cad cang la//rA dang rtsa sdebs ra dang ga/sde ba te ra gnyis pa yis spras//muM sne muM sni nan nan zhes//u aM dang u dang aM/sa gnyis yang na i i gnyis//mtshan te de nas rag mo/
9a3('a): ni/rag mo ni dang kha lo rbad/oM e ong i o ste yang /klu yul du yang spang ba yin//dug sni dug sni kha lo rbad/u dang e au//sa gnyis na yis sna spras pa/ra la bas mtshan o cig ni/klu'i yul du spangs pa yin//kyi kha le dang mu kha lo//lo
9a4('a): gnyis snying la spad re'o//kA la yas brgyan i yis spras//oM dang ra la ba yis mtshan//u dang o dang ra la ga klu yul o o gnyis po spangs//dre'u sna ma'i smod pa ni//rtsa sti rtsa sti rbad ces pa//ra la tsas spras pa la ha//i dang ldan
9a5('a): ba gong ma sbag/de nas ral bas mtshan pa'o//ni rting dang ni rti rbang //i sti sa snA tas mdzes pa//i dang de ldan gong ma chag/tha ma spyad pas sngags dang ldan//mug mug zhes pa de sna ma yi//u dang u dang gnyis kyi sgras//ldem drug ma'i

9b1('a): par byas//de nas yang ni thang mu le/u dang e bris mtshan pa'o//tha mar chad chad gcad pa gnyis//rtsis las ma ra ya la rtsis rbad gnyis gong du ming dang srog/mu sti mu sni nan nan ces//u dang de dang ang dang ldan//sa sna nas mdzes snying du i/

9b3('a): baT kyi kha lo dang kyi kha lo//tha mar rbad ces pa ste ka la yas mdzes kla du i//o dang de 'drar 'og mar sbag/gcang gnyis pa la bas log legs//rtsa ti rtsa ti rbad rbad ces//ra'i 'og du rtsa yis spras//sa la ta ste daM la i//'og tu skrag ste gcad pa

10b3('a): kha rag/snying kha rag dang b+h+yo zhes pa//ye shes lnga ste dzI la ra/sa la nya te e dang yang //de nyid drangs ste srog dang brdzi//ma ra ya dang thun yau sbyar/kham bu dgur yang bsngags pa yin/ma ra ya dang thun la sbyang //kham bu rgya'i spyi bsgrub yin//'phrin

10b5('a): khA mu tra dang rag mo dzA dzaM/de nas dza ti u ta me//u ho mdun du khrag la zlas/kha la ma hi rag+hA b+h+yo/de nas ragau sha i dang srog/de nas g.ya su khrag las so//trag rag sha mu trag mu tra snying kha rag ta la me te u ta me/ur+tha me sa s+thAH/

11a1('a): //shar ro//oM dang ru ru ro ru zhes ye shes lnga ste u u o//u ldan lho'i byur la zlas//ru rU de nas rag sha na//u u gnyis kyi 'og tu legs//dbus kyi thun la zlas pa yin//ru dang rag shwa zhes//u dang myes smras
11a2('a): g.ya su bzlas//ru lu wu rag sha zhes//u u u us 'og tu legs//g.yon gyis phyogasu 'bang ba'i sngags//ru ru ro ru ru lu zhes//tha mar nI ga sha sbyar byas nas//u u o u u yis spras// sman kham bu'i spyi bsgrubs/
11a3('a): thog mAr trag rag shaM zhes brjod//tha mar snying khrag snying b+h+ye zhes//yu ta sU tA b+h+yo zhes tau//ta mes spras pa snre la nya//de la i ti kha la me/pa nya i te de la sogs/ba la pas sgras de la u//de 'dir drangs te srog mtha' can//dr-i ru s+t+r-i kham

11a7('a): gsum pa bdun te bzhi pa bcu//dang po lnga la gnyis pa bdun//gsum pa bdun te bzhi po la//sngogs chen gsum po de laM yang //dang po lnga li de 'og lnga//gsum pa lnga te bzhi pa drug//dang po dgu la bcu gnyis 'og/
11b1('a): gsum bzhi pa dag du'o//bzhi pa'i dang po drug pa ste//gnyis pa drug la gsum pa drug/bzhi pa lnga pa nyid du'o//'phags pa rdo rje nag mo dbang phyug ma'i sngags rgyud las//thun sngags bsgrub pa'i na ya brtsi ba'i le'u ste/drug pa'o//
11b2('a): de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//drag po las kyi bya ba bshad//oM ni ye shes lnga ldan pa/ru ru ke'u ri dbang bskur ba//u u gnyis kyis 'og du legs//ro ru snying khwa brgyad dang ldan//oM uM ldan te tshal pa la//ral pa cig ma'i
11b3('a): rgyu yis//a shug me te u u gnyis//gsang ba'i mdun g.yog dang bcas//me hUM ma b+h+yo dza dza zhes//he la u te aM dang ldan//maN+Dal lho'i sgo ma bzhi//ma mo'i srog te dbang phyug ldan//ma gnyis yis g.yog mo gnyis//
11b4('a): dza ste lnga bcu rtsa brgyad 'gug/de nas dzaM te ral cig ma//g.yog dang bcas pa bcug po dgugs//bcu bdun sngags kyi phreng ba ste//gdul ba'i don 'thun rtag pa yin//oM ni mngon rdzogs yis ldan//ru ru srin mo tsha ba'i
11b5('a): na rtsa la ye'i bsgrub ming ste/ma mo bdun gyi a shug me//hUM zhes thams cad bsdud byed la//b+h+yo zhes thaMd las la bskul//de nas dze yis yang dag 'gugs//gnyis pa bsgrub pa'i ngo bo 'gugs//rgyal ba rnams kyi byin rlabs

12a1('a): he ta ya me//ta me brgyad pa re ma Ni//ru ru ro ru kha zhes pa//u u o u bzhi po ni//khri sman zla khaM bri ba'i sngags//ru ru ro ru mun+na zhes//u u o u u ta ta me//drug pa dwilu sna zhes pa'o//dur ma tI ma du b+h+yo//
12a2('a): u i u te srog dang ldan//ma mo nag mo 'khor te stong //bdun dang ldan pas zlas par bya//trag rag sha dang kha mun+na//b+h+yo zhes ru lu ru lu s+t+waM//ta me u ta ta la me//srog dang u u u u dang //e ka tse ti yang rje mo//
12a3('a): drag rag sha dang snying khrag snying //b+h+yo zhes ta me sa sna nya//de la i te kha la me//sa nya de i ma mo srog/mtha' can re me tau'i sngags//sU ta sU ta b+h+yo zhes pa las//u yis ngo skyes gnyis te sr-ig/khi sman drag po'i sngags zhes pa//
12a4('a): sha sha trag dang sha shos trag/de ginyis 'og tu de dang 'dra//e ka dza Ti drag po sngags//trag mun+te ba re ma ti//ta gnyis mes mtshan bar du u /las kun byed ma ra ya/thun yang sbyar te sgo nga drug/suM tsu rtsa drug dril la zlas//phag/
12a5('a): thod la sogs kun la 'jug/ta gnyis mes mtshan dri'u sna ma//tra ga dang snying khrag ces//'jug tu snying khag te la me//o ta te me pa nya me/is sbrad la 'ang ma yis mtshan//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//ma ma drag dang rag/
12a6('a): sha zhes//de 'og mu tra kha mu tra//tra me u Na mes smras pa//u te ta la mes mtshan bya//mu ru mu ru mu ru zhes//u u drug gis 'og du legs//b+h+yo b+h+yo b+h+yo zhes 'jug du gdags//srog gnyis bar du au u AM/trag zhes
12a7('a): rag sha kha mun+na//tra me u me ta la'ang mo//dur mi ti te rag sho mo//tsaM zhes u i o gsuM brgyan//ma ma dur Ni u 'ig legs//dum lo na ya u oMs mchin//snying zhes kha la ragra brjod//so la nya s+t+waM is sprad pa/

12b2('a): me he ba thum ste ai u gnyis//du du thu pra raM zhes pa//u gsum skyabasu pa la mAM//du lon yang 'jug tu te//u o gnyis kyi mdzes par bya//i u a dang sa ma yA//thibs thibs ling shar yang nges song //sa ma ya
12b3('a): zhes tiSh+Ta ni//i u i gsum e au zhes//i ste sna tA pa yis mdzes//sgro'i thun sngags yin par bshad//ma ma ma ru thibs thibs zhes//u i i gsum rgyan du byas//tha ba pra bur dang kha shar 'am//kha la me

12b5('a): phye dang lcags sgrog 'khrol//oM ldan pa la ma te e//la 'og tsa te sa sU ga/de la me o kha ma au//mi ring song+ge i o ldan//badz+ra ne te phadzo gzhug brjod//dzaM la me ldan e rgyan can//ma ra ya dang thun ya gdags//
12b6('a): sgo nga rtsa cig thun sngags yin//badz+ra tai phaT/badz+re ra tre phaT/dzA me o dz+r-i me es brgyan//badz+ra e hi e hi zhes//dza me e i ai is brgyan/na ga le dang na ga le/e gnyis steng du rgyan pa'o//suM pa li dang suM pa le//u
12b7('a): e u es brgyan du byas//tsin yang cin yang zhes pa ni//i i gnyis kyis brgyan pa'o//kho na yang len yang zhes pa ni//e es steng nas mtshan par byas//kha khaM ba mo dang sh+ma ba ma//aM aM gnyis dang ldan pa/
13a1('a): ma dung dbyu bo ma rung b+h+yo/u dang rdzogs dang u sgrog ldan//brgyad dang bcu bdun cig ldan pa/sgong dug dang khrag dang drug/thaMng kun lam bsngags pa yin//'phags pardo rje nag mo dbang phyug ma'i sngags rgyud pas//
13a2('a): rtsa ba'i sngags dang zor thun tha~Md kyin ma rtsamA pa'i le'u ste/bdun pa'o///de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba//ma hA rag+na tsit+ta zhes//i te drug ldan gsang ba'i sngags//ka ma la dang ragra zhes//si ti tsit+ta i i i//dgu pa'i yi
13a3('a): yin na ya'o//gu h+ya rag shA tsit+ta zhes//u i yi ge drug pa yin//hUM baM sha tsit+ta tsit+ta//dbang dang aM te i ai bdun//ma ma tse bur tsit+ta dar sna tsa ldan pa bshod//pu ru t+ye mas tsit+ta zhes//u u la na tas us brgyun//i zhes drug pan+d+han
13a4('a): can no thun sngags drug gi ces bya bar bshad//ma hA kha T+wAM a bi ra//tsi tsi zhes kha la wa//e gnyis yi ge bcu pa'o//ha tra dang nI ma hA drang ke ru lu tsit+ta ta me gnyis//u u i zhes yi ge dgu/brgyad kyi tha mar srog tu tung //bskul ba'i
13a5('a): sngags 'di sbyar bshad pa'o//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba/bod dang chad dang bsad pa'i sngags//drag rag sha dang mu tra ya snying kha rag ma b+h+yo b+h+yo zhes//ta mo gnyis te dbusu u/san nya ste da la i//ma mo'i srog snying ginyis
13a6('a): mtha' can//rbang sngags yi ge bcu gnyis pa'o//trag rag shu dang mun+na/ru lu ru lu hUM b+h+yo hUM//tA gnyis me te dbu su u//u bzhi ho l-i u a ma can//bar du srog taM bcu gsum pa'o//rag mo tre keng tsa che/ga la b+h+yos
13a7('a): e e dang srog//dgu dang ldan par bshad pa'o//ru lu ru lu ma Ni b+h+yo//u bzhi e sreg bdun po yin//drag rag sha dang mu tra kha mu tra rag mo dza dza zhes//Na gnyis me te dbu su u//u o bar du te mes spras//oM ye
13b1('a): bcu gnyis gsang ba'o//hi khaM kha la ma b+h+yo zhes//e u srog ste drug pa yin//bod pa zhes bya ba'i sngagaso//trag rag sha dang mu dza snying /kha rag rbad bruM ri la li zhes/ri li b+h+yo twe te me gnyis//dbusu u te sa s+yAhA/

13b4('a): spras pa dgu par bshad//lo shi kha la rag sha dang /rbad na ba hUM ste o i gtsang //u aM hes spras de ya dgu/du na sa ra thim na dgra bo hUM//u i u aM mdzes//yi ge bdun pa dag du bshad//'phags pa rdo rje nag mo dbang
13b5('a): phyug ma'i rgyud las khong dang rbang gsad pa dang //khar nag rgyun gyi le'u ste brgyud pa'o///de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//spyan drangs mchod dang stong pa kun//snying po 'di yis bya bar bshad//nag mo tI lamadun b+h+yo/
13b6('a): b+h+ya/ka li dun+ta r+g+ho dun d+d+he spyan drangs pa'o//ka li ragA ma du nI ma ma ya hUM//phyag go//rag sha ma hri rgyal pad+ma sa ma ya tany+dzA na/badzin par//ma mo ka li ba de d+ho li hU~M d+ha ta ma la kha hU~M//rak+ShA Sha ti/ban+d+ha a myaM tA
13b7('a): tshong pa'o//ma mo stong ga tshol pa//du ni tal du rnyi byi b+he//las btsamo //drag rag sha ma hi dur b+ho khyer/srig snying du shes rag mo ga yom pa du nI nyer mi hud cang ba'o//badz+ra kun chad thod pa'o//snying tra/
14a1('a): /che la ba rbad rA skyabs tsit+ta rbad/myags mugs rbad/snying khrag kha la rbad/tsha la du se ni si 'du rbud/hu ra thums srog la rbad/phur pas gadrab pa'i sngagaso//rba du rba du yag mo sod cig ni dza/
14a2('a): thum ri li li nI dza/myur du bsgral ba'i snying po'o//jag r-ig shad mu tra snying kha rag ma b+h+yo//mdun phung gtor ba'o//yaM gr-i ni ya ma/sa rog ma ti rbad yam/bi li ma ti yi/dkrug pa'o//raMg mo ni shA/lu tsaM tse ma
14a3('a): rbad/rag mo kha la tsa tsa b+h+yAM b+h+yo//tsa sti rbad snying la dun/rAg mo kha la tsa tsa duna? b+h+yo b+h+yo//kur ma me sham b+h+yo//rbad pa'o//da ni bsad cing mnan pa bshad par bya//pha mes thod pa ming rus/dkar na gi dkris byas la/ the

37a7('a): log pa'i bar tshong bdung//nga rgyal khengs pa'i gshin rje dang //gti mug myos pa'i srin mo rnams//srid pa gsum la dbang che te//laM sum ma lus rims kyis sdus//nga rgyal drag po'i gzugs 'chang bas//zug dpung che la dgongs pa rags//nga ro che zhing gtu mo phog can/lag
37b1('a): na mtshon cha sna tshogs thogs//byams dang snying rje'i lam dang bral//che ba can dregs pa 'dzin pa'i mthus//log pa'i lam dang grol ba'i sa//phyin ci log la yid ches pas//las su mtshan mchos la spyod//srid pa'i sa bon rtsub mo 'phangs//dmyal ba'i sdug bsngal nyams
37b2('a): myong 'gal te de las thar nas kyang //slar yang srin po'i lus blangs nas//gong ma bzhin du 'khor bar byed//de nyid 'khor kyang srin mo dang //ma mo dang ni mkha'agro ma//shin tu gtum pa'i 'khor dang bcas//bsod pa'i las la 'bags shing grims//sha dang khrag dang srog
37b3('a): lo spyod//nges pa'i lung dang ma ldan pas//kun kyang dmyal ba'i lam du gyur//sbrul sku la sogs sangs rgyas kyis//'dul ba'i las su ma gyur te//slar yang khengs pa rgol bar byed//stug pos brgyan pa'i zhing khams nas//rgyal ba rnams kyi thugs kyi gzigs//rdo rje sem
37b4('a): dpa' stan las langs//kun bzang yab la 'di skad gsol//e ma bcom ldan a la la//rgyal ba'i zhing khams 'di dag na//log pa'i bdud dang srin po rnams//sangs rgyas bka' las 'gal bgyid pa//'di la nan tan ji ltar bgyi//kun bzangs lha'i bka' stsal pa/
37b5('a): bdud dang gshin rje srin po rnams//nga rgyal dregs pas bcings pa la//shin tu gdug cing ga tu ma byed pas//de la zhi bas phran mi 'gyur//shes rab thabs dang ldan byas nas//khro bo de bzhin gshegs pa mdzad//sangs rgyas thugs rjes khros pa'i//stong gsum ma lus thaMd
37b6('a): kyang skad cig yud la med 'gyur na//srid pa gsum po slod ci dgos//dur khrod gar gyi phyag rgyar bskyed//de nas ston pa rdo rje 'dzin//legs par thal sbyar phyag 'tshal nas//de nyid las ni gnas gyur te//gdug cing drag la rtsub pa'i//gong ma las kyang cher snang zhing //
37b7('a): dbu dang phyag zhabs mang po dang //mtshon che rnam pa sna tshogs pa//'jigs pa'i cha lugs mang pos brgyan//gzi byin mthu dpung skye du med//rab tu rngam zhing thugs gtum nas//dgyes mdzad rdo rje gsor nas kyang //goms pa'i stobs kyi gar byed pa//sku'i sprul pa sna tshogs/
38a1('a): @/rdzas kyis tha 'jig rten gsum po ba bkang nas//sring ba gsum yang dus gcig g.yos/nyi ma 'dzum gyi 'ong gsal bas//dregs bkun kyang tshig cing 'dros//'jug stong mang po'i sgra bsgrags pas//gdug pa ma lus rgyal zhing skrag/sman chang sdom zhes bya ba dang
38a2('a): 'dra su mchi zhes gros bor kyang //thugs rje'i de'i khar ma gtang //srin po'i chung ma srin mo gtum//smug nag kro ti shwa ri de//dpal chen 'bar ba'i thugs rje yis//phyag rgya ba ni pos dbang du srung //thabs dang shes rab rol pa las//byang chub sems kyi byin brlabs kyis//
38a3('a): dpal chen rak+Sha he ru ka dang //rngam pa'i tshul gyis phyir thon te//sku che yan lag rags pa dang //zhal dgrang mche ba gtsigs pa yis//'jig rten khams kun gang byas shing //bdud dang gshin rje srin po dang //gdug pa ma lus bkrag par mdzad//'jigs pa'i gdong pa
38a4('a): 'jigs pa'i gzugs//'jigs pa'i mtshon cha sna tshogs kyi//pha rol dregs pa mtshar bcad nas//shin tu bkrag cing rgyal bar byas//rab tu 'bar ba'i ma dpung dang //sreg pa'i me chen rab sbar te//pha rol g.yul las rgyal bar mdzad//srin po'i chung ma la sogs
38a5('a): rnams//phyag rgya bzhi yi dbang du sdus//thabs dang shes rab rol pa mdzad//zhal nas gdug pa'i tshig 'di brjod//nga ni shin tu sgrib shing sgyums//nga ni sangs rgyas nyin shwa can//dus gsum sangs rgyas zos pas ngam 'grangs//dud 'gro kun ze skyes la bsgom//'jig
38a6('a): rten lha mi 'ga' tsam bsad pa la'o//bsod ma 'jig su rung zhes skrag/'jig byed nga lta bu la 'di skad bya//gang gi bsod par 'dod kyang mi gto //rab tu rngams pa'i tshig de bos tsam gyis//bdud la sogs te srin po thams cad kun//rab tu sgregs te tshig du 'di skad
38a7('a): smras/mi byed thong thong rgyal po mi byed thong /ci ste sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun 'am//snyom pa'i snying rje khyod la mi mngar 'am/yun du 'angs bgyid nges pa'i bros dor zhes/gdung bas khas len sgra yang ma btang ste//gzir mnon phyag rgya chen pos ma btang ste/'angar zhig/
38b1('a): thar med drung na nyen cing 'khor//de'i 'khor dang smras dang bran du bcas//'dar zhing skrag nas gcer bu bzhin du gnas//phra men sbyang ki las kyi rgyal po'i rgyud las/rjen 'byung ba'i gling bzhi'i le ru ste/dang pe'o//bcom ldan 'das dpal he ru ka la phyag 'tsha'o//
38b2('a): 'das pa'i dus na sngon byung ba/bdud dang gshin rje dbang phyug de//dpal chen yAg ho ru kas//thugs rje tshad med bzhi bsgral te//ro'i nyal thabs gan rgyal du/'dzam gling lce'i gling kham su//khor nas sa bzhir gyur pa ni//dur khrod chen po brgya du gyur//mge ni lho nub mtsham
38b3('a): su bstam//lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyis brkyang te//mgo dang lus snying yan lag ni//dur khrid brgyad du bstan pa las//thugs rje chen po'i byin brlabs kyis//dur khrod gnas su sbyang ba'i phyir//re'i bsrungs mtshan po ni//ma mo chen po brgyad banago//de'i gnas
38b4('a): rten chen mo yin//mchod rten po gzi ldan can//rang byung re re bzhag pa yin//de'i lus snying ma ga ta//dur khrod chen po bsil ba'i tshal//ma mo chen mo kau ri ma//mkhas shing snying stobs che ba'i sa//mchod rten ming ni bde byed legs//de'i mgo ni lho phyogs kyi//
38b5('a): dur khrod chen po bzhag pa yin//sing+ga yul gyi gling phran yin/ma mo chen mo tsau ri ma//mthong thos rnam par 'phrul bag mnga'//mchod rten ri bo t+ta na la//lag pa g.yas pa shar lho mtshams//dur khrod chen po bal yul yin//ma mo chen mo pu ka yis//lhag par
38b6('a): zas kyi longs skyed che//mchod rten kha sho byA khri kha//lag pa g.yon pa lho nub mtshams//dur khrod chen po sa la'i yul//ma mo chen mo be ta li//phyag na rdo rje dbang gi gnas//de na mthu dang dbang yang che//gyang yul mchod rten go ma la//rkang pa g.yas pa
38b7('a): byang shar mtshams//dur khrod chen po li'i yul//ma me chen mo tsaN+Da li//lha mo shangs kyi ni zhes grags/chung med brub rdzu 'phrul can//mchod rtsen ming ni 'gi'u ti sham/rkang pa g.yon pa byang shar mtshams//dur khrod chen po kha che yul//ma mo chen mo pra mo/
39a1('a): /ha bde sgying ldan pa phun sum tshogs//mcho rten ming ni ka ni ka/'o mtshan steng gi byang phyogs na//dur khrod chen po u rgyun yul//ma mo chen mo g+ha smA ra/'byor pa'i longs spyod lnga dang ldan//'os grub can yang den mang //gzi'i mchod rten chen po
39a2('a): ste ming yang d+harma tsakra to//de'i nang grol thams cad kun//dur khrod chen po za ho ra yu la//rgya mtsho chen po rlung gi ma hA de//lang ka pu rang srin mo'i yul//ma mo chen mo sme shi ni//srin po srin mo sha zla ma//de dag kun la dbang bsgyur zhing //rin chen nor gyi dpag bsam shing
39a3('a): 'byor ba longs spyod kun dang ldan/mchod rten bde byed gno mu nis//phra men sbyang ki las kyi rgyal po'i rgyud las//'jig rten du bstan pa'i dur khrod chen po'i gnas brgyad kyi gleng blang ba'i le'u ste/gnyis pa'o///de nas 'jig byed chen po'i dkyil 'khor
39a4('a): bskyed/dang por de bzhin nyid kyi tingange 'dzin chos kyi dbyings spros pa dang bral bar bsgo mo//de nas kun snang gi tingange 'dzin ni//kun gzhi byang chub sems mtshan ma'i spros pa dang bral bar bsgo mo//de nas rgyu'i ting nge 'dzin ni/sems kyi sa bon hUM nag po gcig tu mngon sum du gsal
39a5('a): bar bsgre mo//de nas 'di skad ces bskulo//skye mched rnam dag chos kyi dbyings//sgros dang bral bas de bzhin nyid//mi rtog mi dmigs byang chub sems//gzung 'dzin dag pa kun tu snang //yi ge hUM ni sems kyi rgyu yin te//mtha' dbus med par kun tu khyab par dmigs//spros
39a6('a): bral hUM ni 'bar ba'i skur bstan pa'o//hU~M nag 'bar ba'i sku la phyag 'tshalo//de nas hU~M mthing nag 'bar ba de mdun gyi bar snang la gnas pa de la//phar spros pas rgyal ba rnams kyi thugs ngam bskul//slar hUM la 'dus/hUM las skur gyur to//de'i khyad par las/'og gi
39a7('a): phyogs su yi ge/raM yaM khaM/kau bruM/bsam mo//yi ge de lnga las 'phros 'dus pa'i tshogs dbang chen gyi ngal bskyed/de nas gzhal yas khang bskyed//gzhal yas khang gi dbusu/dal bskyed pa dang gdan gding ba gzhung dang mthun//de nas mkha' dbyings yi ge hUM gnas
39b1('a): 'di dag gdan la dbab bo//hUM nI bar snang gnas pa las/drag po'i ting 'dzin 'bar ba yis//gdan gyi steng du bab nas kyang //'bar ba'i gdan la brtan par bzhugs//oM hUM hUM man+da la ha la ha la hUM dzaM zhes brjod pas/yi ge hUM de las babs te/de nas sku
39b2('a): bskyed pa 'di skad brjod do//oM oM hUM swA hA/hUM gi 'bar ba'i cho 'phrul las//hUM mthing nag byung ste/de nas hUM la 'di skad bskulo//hUM las ye shes 'bar ba'i 'od 'phros pas//'phrin las rnam bzhi sku la rdzogs mdzad pas//'khros nas dbang sdud 'bar ba'i skur bstan
39b3('a): pas sku mchog sprul pa'i he ru'i sku byung ngo //hU~M hUM ya gu he ru ka ma hA badz+ra sarba duSh+TAn ta ka ha na dA ha pa tsa hUM phaT/yak+Sha kro ti shwa ri hUM phaT/a la la ho//zhes brjod pas he ru ka'i sku mngon du byung ste/sku mdog mthing nag zhal gsum phyag drug pa/zhabs bzhi bskrad pa
39b4('a): /g.yon na yum yAk+sha 'khros pa'i rgyal mo gtso bo dang 'dre bar bskye //de nas yab yum dgyes pra'ing skad //sku gsung thugasu byin rlabs nas//de nas yum gyi mkha' dkyil du//dga' mchog rdo rje rab gsor nas//ye shes sems dpa'i cho 'phrul bstan//shar
39b5('a): phyogs grags pa bsgral ba'i phyir//sems dpa'i khyi chung dkar por sprul//srin mo rta gdong lcags kyu la//thabs dang shes rab sbyar ba las//dbang sdud rab tu mngon gsal to//de nas dbang du 'dus pa la//khro bo zhal gsum phyag drug sbrul//ma mo gau ri sngon mo byung
39b6('a): dgyes pa'i sgra ni ha ha bshad//yAgrA he ru ka'i dbang bskur nas//gsing byed bdag mo yin par gsal//srin po ma TaM bsgral ba'i phyir//gdug pa'i mtshon cha spu gri 'bar//me'i 'dzin byed lcags kyus gzung //bud med sdug pa'im tshon cha yis//srin po ma Ta
39b7('a): dkar po sgral//ma mo gI ri ma'i gnas//de na dur khrod bdag po dag/ro lang spyang po'i bdag po dang //yum ni lha mo gI raM gnyis//thabs dang shos rab sbyar ba las//mngon 'byung si he seng+ge'i gdong //pho nya yin pa dbang bskuro//yAg+h+ra shri he ru dang //yAgra 'khros pa'i rgyal mi gnyis/
40a1('a): //thabs dang shes rab sbyar ba las//mngon 'byung sems dpa' mthing ka'i sku/srin mo dag gdong zhags pa ma/yang snga rab tu mngon gsapa te//thabs dang shes rab sbyar ba las//blo bonal gsum phyag drug sbrul//mngon byung ma mo gau ri ma//dgyes pa/

40b1('a): sted dril bus bkug d+hA//dwe ta nag me'i spu gri yis//ra t+ya nag po sprul ba'i byed//khog pa dur khrod gzhal yas khang //ma mo b+h+re ta li'i gnas//de nas dur khrod bdag po yis//srin po rAm+b+ha /sbyang mgo dang //ma mo chen po b+h+re tra gnyis//thabs pa/
40b2('a): gnyis med las//sngon 'byung shwa na sbyang mgo can//dgyes pa'i sgra skad ha he bzhad //phra men ma ru dbang bsku ro//phrin las rdzogs pa'i sku la ni//oM bsod byed rngam pa'i sku phyung ste//rag+sa shri he ru dang //yAg 'khros pa'i rgyal mo gnyis//thabs dang shes rab gnyis med las//sngon//

41a1('a): #/yas kyang //mtshon gyi zhags pas bzung nas ni//spu gris snying chung ston pa'i//ma mo sma sha ni'i gnas//de nas dur khrod bdag po dag/srin po chen po 'byin chen dang //ma mo chen po sma sha gnyis//thabs dang shes rab gnyis med las//sngon byung kha kha pho rog mgo//dgyes/

41a7('a): byed pa//'byung po gtum po nga rgyal tsan//khyed ni sgrib pa ci sgrib//yang dag shes rab ye shes kyis//khungs rtag tsang bcad bdag las byung //bdag 'dzin bdag gir 'dzin pa ni//bdag med stong pa'i tho bas gzhom//bdag po thabs stobs brtson 'grus kyi//gnas ngan len pa'i ded dpon bye/
41b1('a): de nyid bdag rtogs byed pas na//bdag med steng pa'i tho bas gzhom//ang ma ni/d+hi ma hA dz+nyA na badz+ra/mu kA lA/a ko te ya hUM phaT/sarba pa pa kar ta/ka ra pa si/ma traM kha ru tra hUM phaT/chos chen dag gi go gon te//dpal chen stobs kyang che'i bdag/stong pa'i mtshon cha sngon po yis//de dag smad pa dag
41b2('a): /kyang 'gas//chos rnam rtag tu rang gi ngo bos stong //rgyal ba'i sras rnams dngos por rnam par 'jig/srid pa ji bzhin yod med stong pa yin//de nas yang 'di skad ces brjod//'phags pa thams cad dgongasu gsol//'byung po mang po kun nyon cig/rdo rje rnon po 'jam dpal gyis//thugs rje de dang shes
41b3('a): kyis//thabs la mkhas pa'i byin brlabs kyis//'gro ba thams cad gzung ba mdzod//'gro ba'i bdag po tshe dang srog/gson por bde bar gcod pa dang //bdag dang gshin rje 'khor bcas la//'joms shing gcod pa'i gshed po ni//'jam dpal 'phrul gyi dngos grub mchog/srid gsum
41b4('a): thar byed rngam pa de//khwa dang thong byug mi zad pas//snying rjes bcom nas bsad par bya//dz+nyA hUM baM ho shrI he ru ka badz+ra sarba duSh+TA na sa ma yA mu tra/pra ban tsa ti kra/tseg she ka ha na hUM phaT/bdag po'i rol thab drag po yi//ro gnyis nyal thabs gan kyal ru//mgo ni lho nub mtsham su bstan
41b5('a): rkang pa byang shar mtshams nas kyang //lag pa g.yas pa'i 'khrig ma la//khyi gnyis dang ni bya rgod gnyis//rkang pa ta cung zad sgrol ba la//thal ba'i mdog la sku 'greng ba//khog pa na mkha' gzhal yas khang //yan lag dur khrod rin chen gter//shes rab dgra stas bshag par
41b6('a): nga gra sta'i phyag rgyas bshag par bya ba yino//de ltar khog pa bshags nas/brum ngar kha ga la si/zhes brjod pas/khog pa nang du gzhal yas khang du gyur par bas mo//de'i nang du bzhugs pa ni//gnas kyi sgo 'di su yi bcad//sems kyi bdag pos bcang dam ci//bzung

56a1('a): ging dang bse'i skyes bu bstan//bdud dang bshin rje ma ru rtse//de lta bu'i 'khor tshogs dang //rang bzhin thabs gcig bzhugs pa'i tshe//rang bzhin 'khor ni shugs kyi 'dul//las kyi 'khor ni sprul pas 'dul//
56a2('a): bsrung byed 'khor ni thabs kyis 'dul//sgro lo byad 'khor ni rol pas 'dul//de'i 'dul thabs 'di lta ste//skye med mi g.yo chos kyi dbyings//rang bzhin shugs kyis 'dul thabs so//snang srid rig pa'i cho 'phrul gyis//snyig ma'i ma mo 'jig
56a3('a): bar 'gyur//gnyis med sras kyis rol pa yis//sku gsung thugs kyi ma mo 'dzag/rig pa gar thabs sna tshogs kyi//shan pa'i dpung rnams 'jom pa'i dpal//mngon spyod rig pa'i brtul shugs kyis//srid pa'i pho rgyud mtshan mar ma
56a4('a): 'dul thab lnga los ye shes shar//gsal la mi rtog ye shes dang //mnyam la ma 'dres ye shes dang //be dag 'byed pa'i ye shes dang //phrin las rdzogs pa'i ye shes dang //lhun gyis grub pa'i ye shes dang /'khor las 'khrul
56a5('a): pa 'di lta ste/dang po lha dang gnyis pa mi//gsum pa yi dwags yin pa ste//bzhi pa byol song gnas rigs la//lnga pa dmyal ba'i 'khrul 'khor lo'o//rtogs pa'i sa rnams 'di lta ste//tha ma sgral bas dang po ste//kun du 'ong du shar ba'o//
56a6('a): bzhi pa gnyis pa'i don du'o//pad+ma can du gnang ba'o//gsum pa gnas su gol ba'o//'khor lo cho gas chen don du ldan//gnyis pa dag pa bzhi pa la//ye shes bla ma non pa'o//gcig la lnga'i don du te//rdzogs pa'i spyi cig don du shar/
56a7('a): 'khor lnga smin pa'i 'bras bu lnga//dang po sa'i 'od yin ste//gnyis pa me'i 'od du 'bar//gsum chu'i ma dang su ldan//bzhi pa skyong pa'i thabs ya na te//lnga pa na mkha'i ye shes so//dang po rtsa la gnyis pa khrag/gsum pa dbugs
56b1('a): la bzhi pa drod//lnga pa bkrag gi do ba'o//'khor gyis bsdu ba 'di lta ste//dpal chen thugs kyi thig le rdzogs//hUM snying rje chen pos te//'a ni byams pa'i rang bzhin la/ha ni dga' ba chen po ste//zla tshes btang snyoms bdag nyid bsam//
56b2('a): da ni brtson 'grus 'byung ba la//a ni shes rab chen po'i don//ma zhar bsdus pas mya ngan zlas//mi dmigs chos kyi dbyings su thim//rdo rje mkha' 'gro'i rgyud las glang gzhi dang 'khor mya ngan las bzla ba'i le'u ste/dang po'o//
56b3('a): /rgyal mtshan rim pa gang lags pa//ngo mtshar lha la bdag nyid 'tshal//rdzogs sangs rgyas kyis bka' stsal pa/dben la gnyan pa'i chu klung du/ma mo 'du ba'i dur khrod du//gnod sbyin 'du ba'i mdung rtse dang /srin mo 'du ba'i sbar bshad du/
56b4('a): spel dang 'gugs dang dadog pa dang //mnan dang sba ba de bzhin no//mchod dang skad pa'i rdzas rnams dang //bum lnga rtan du bcas pas te//khru bzhi sbyangs pa'i maN+Dal du//dbang gi skud pas phye nas ni//rdul byed lnga dang ldan par bya//dbus
56b5('a): su zla kham sgo bzhi la//dbang gi dkyil 'khor gsum gyis brgyan/bad bzhi 'khor lo rtsibs brgyad ldan//snam bu sgo yis rnam par mdzes//tshe mchog yab yum gsal byas te//thugs las bum pa zlum gyis rlabs//nyi zla steng du ha/
56b6('a): lnga dgod//sgo bzhir glu 'debs snying po dgod//rgyal mo lnga'i snying po skyed//lha mo lnga'i dkyil 'khor la//sgo ma bzhi'i mdzes par bskyed//gtso bo thugs las 'od 'phros pas//bskul bas dzaHdang nri ru drangs//yab yum snyan pa
56b7('a): khal ru spros//za sna la mar drangs pas 'od du gsal//yab la rigs lnga dbu la rdzogs//yum lnga mkha' la shar ba'o//dkyil 'kho bzhal ni bsgyur byas la//zhi ba nub sgo sha 'dus te//rig bzhi ldan pa'i dgur rdzogs pa//'ad ba
57a1('a): mu khyung 'ab mdzes pa'o//da tshe phyogs bzhir skyed pa ba//ye shes gtum mo bzhi pa dang /gzhad pa'i rgyal ma bzhi 'ogasal//zung bzhi dgyed snga rab tu bsam//sgo bzhi gru bdegs pas ma byas te//'khar
57a2('a): ma mo bcu drug dbus//yum gyi gnam zhal gdengs pa ru//thig le btsud kyi thabs 'tshams su//nyi zla 'bar ba'i ga'u ru//rje'i rtse la ha lnga bsam/de ni rgyal mo lnga'i rgya//de steng rta mgrin dbang gi rgya//rdo rje rgya gram 'bar ba'i rgyu//rA la/
57a3('a): nyi zla 'bar ba'i rgya//de nas sngags kyi pho nya rgya//yab yum sngags la las sngags gdags//oM shriHhe ru ka/kr-i d+hi shwa ri phaT/nI a yu sha dz+wa/gtso'i thugs las 'od 'phros pas//bcu drug ma mo rgyud bskul te//tshe ste na nI ru/
57a4('a): drangs nas su//gtso bo thugs la bsdus byas pas//rdo rje la ma byung rnam zhal mkhas//rta mgrin zhal nas ga'ur 'khyil//tshig gau pho nya dgug byas pa//hUM che zhes chen mo gsang ba'i um//thog le sbyor ba'i yum gcig
57a5('a): maT dkar mo nyi zla zod phre can//gru gsumo 'bar ba'i mig cig ma//ga yu'i ral pas srid pa sgrol//chags pa'i phyag gis pad+ma 'dzin//rin chen mchi ma'i skye 'gro'i bsol//tsit+ta zhag pas mu stegs sgrol//rdzu 'phrul shugs
57a6('a): kyi tshes drongs shig/de nas dbel bar bya ste//bgris mu ran hUM a yu She sa hUM a//a b+hi sha san+ti a yu She/hUM a bi yang lang te/a yu She hUM a//ma hA lang te/hUM a yu She hUM A/che mchog kun gyi 'phro 'du yis//garba
57a7('a): rgyal mo lnga bskul bas//'phro 'udu nyi ma'i 'od ltar sbal//de nas cho gnas bya ba ni//rgyal mo lnga po rten la sku/hUM mkha' mnyam pa'i ye shes ni//chos dbyings nyi ma lta bu la//'phro 'du ye shes lnga ru shar//bgra chags
57b1('a): lnga'i rgyud sbyangs nas//'byung ba lnga yi bcud bsdus pas//mi 'gyur tshe'i dngos grub thob//bum pa ba shig la tshe dbang bskur//gnon pa'i sngags kyis dral byas la//smin 'tshams sbas pa rgya yis gdag/'brang rgyas bkra shis
57b2('a): gsal byas nas//mkha' 'gro tshogs kyis btang rag btang //rdo rje mkha' 'gro'i rgyud las//rgyal po bsgyur ba'i le'u ste/gnyis pa'o///'phra men rgya mtshos yang gsol po//srid brgyud kyi byung tshul dang //grub
57b3('a): pa'i don ni 'bras bu gnyis//ngo mtshar lha la bdag nyan 'tshal//bcu tshe bzhi rdzogs rgyal stsogs te/lha mo yongs khyud bde ba 'thas//bka' don gtan tshigs gtan la phab//mi nub tshe'i gtan tshigs dang //rtogs pa don gyi
57b4('a): ba dang //sa gzhi lta bu can 'phrang dang //rgyal po lta bu'i bka' nyan dang /mdzad pa thabs kyi gar thabs dang //gsal ba don gyi dkyil 'khor dang //lhun gyi grub pa'i bsgrub pa dang //mngon spyod las kyi mtha' rgya rnams//nga'i gsang
57b5('a): rgyud chen po'i mdo//bzhi gnyis don du ngas bstan gyis//'kho ra gyi gtso bo sems gtod cig/brjod bya brjod byed thabs tshul gyis//dgos dang dgos pa'i bye brag dang //'brel pa thabs kyi gtan tshigs rnams//'brel don
57b6('a): gtan tshigs gtan la phab//rdo rje khro bo'i rgyud las/gleng blangs pa'i le'u ste/gsum pa'o///rtogs pa'i don ni 'di lta ste//skye med mi gnas gzung 'dzin bral//ma skyes phyir na skye ba med//dus gsum
57b7('a): po 'gyur med pa'i don//mi g.yo sems kyi bdag nyid la//'pho 'gyur med pa'i rig pa gsal//'byung lnga sems kyi sprul pa la//ye shes lnga'i mtha' rgya cas//kun 'dus snang sring dbyings lo 'dus//'pho 'gyur dus gis
58a1('a): dang du gsal//snang srid ra shar kun du bzang //ye nas ma bshegs rang byung ba//snang sing phag rgya chen po'i don//kun bzang klong gi dkyil 'khor la//rig pa thugs kyi 'od shar ba//mdzes pa yul gyi rgyan
58a2('a): gyis brgyan//don kun rgyal ba'i thugs las rjegase//rgyal ba thugs rje gnyis med//ske 'gag ma lus sems la gas lo//ma gags rig pa'i ye shes la//snod bcu da kun gyi 'od 'dus pa//mkha' la rlung gi don du sha ra//ma ni ngang
58a3('a): gi rang bzhin la//mo ni rigs pa'i don shar ba//rig pa ma 'gags gtan chags kyis//skye med don kun gtan la phabs//skye med don dam tshad ma yis//skye ba'i chos kun gtan la phab//dbyings dang ye shes rang bzhin
58a4('a): las rba snang srid ma mor rang bzhin 'char//rdzu 'phrul shugs kyi mkha' 'gro rnams//rgyal sras thugs kyi dkyil nas byung //rang gis ngang la brtags byas pas//snang dang stong pa'i don du rtogs//rang nyid tshad ma'i rgyal po taM//shar
58a5('a): ba rang nyid stong par rtogs//skye 'gag med pa'i ngang nyid las//rol pa'i khyad par ci yang snang //mtshan ma'i gzung 'dzin sna tshogs kun//sbyin phung mkha' la thim pa bzhin//ma skyes chos dbyings dang du thim//thim pa'i rgyal
58a6('a): po phyag rgya gsal//snang sring rig pa'i rgyan shes na//ma mo kun gyi re gnas yin//srin po'i ma mo thams cad dang /rgyal ba'i thugs las bye brag med//de'i don 'di lta ste//mkha' lnga 'od du gsal rtogs na//'byung ba lnga'i
58a7('a): rnam rtog bral//ma skyes rig pa'i don la bsal//chos nyid 'dzin pa'i mtshan ma 'jig/gser gyi thig le don bsal ba//'phra men rgya mtsho spos dang bral//thig lo chen po'i don rtogs na//bod 'breng ma'i 'dzin rtogs
58b1('a): bral na ye shes bzhi'i don dkar bas//khros pa'i dbang chen mtshan ma 'jig/rnam par shes pa'i ngang rtogs nas//ma chen bzhi'i 'dzin rtogs bral//don dang sems can rig 'gyur na//dge sdig ma yi mya ngan 'das/skyed
58b2('a): pa'i don nyid rtogs 'gyur na//chags byed pa'i mtshan ma 'jig/snyoms byed byams pa'i ngang gnas pa//cha snyoms ma'i mtshan ma 'joms//las slu med pa'i don gsal bas//na mkha'i rgyal mo'i 'dzin rtogs bral//dpag
58b3('a): bsam shing gi gcung phyung bas//bu mo drug gi gti mug gsal//zangs kyi sbu gu'i ngang skyod ma//'dod khams gsum gyi 'dod chags sgrol//rgyu ba rlung gi don shes pas//srid bzhi dmag dpon mtshan ma 'jig
58b4('a): dbang po 'dir bsdus rig gyur na/sring mo bdun gyis zhe sdang 'jig/dga' ba bzhi'i dus shes na//dus bzhi'i rgyal mo rtog pa 'jig/rus pa'i ri rab don bsal na/nyi shu rtsa brgyad mtshan ma 'joms//srid pa mtshan
58b5('a): ma'i las rtog kun//bdag med rtog pa'i thabs kyis 'dul//snang srid snang ba'i ma mo kun/rig pa'i ye shes cho 'phrul te/rig pa mkha' la 'gro ba ni//sa rnams kun du rgyu 'gro ma/rang bzhin gnas pa'i
58b6('a): rdzu 'phrul can//mkha' la 'pho zhing dbyings la nyal//snang srid gar thabs rgyal ba'i yum//'pho 'gyur mi mnga' skye 'gro gsoso//bad chen bcu'i den rtogs pa/rtsa ba byang chub sems su gsal//spyi dpal bcu'i don gsal bas
58b7('a): mngon rdzogs yab kyi thugs las rdzogs//gsang tshan bcu'i mkha' gsal bas//b+ha ga bcu'i dbyingasu 'byung //rdzogs chen bcu'i don gsal bas//rgyal ba'i sku dang ya nas mnyam//mngon rdzogs yab rgya
59a1('a): dkyil 'khor ro//bcu tsho bcu'i rgyan gyis brgyan//tshogs thugs kyi dkyil 'khor la//ye shes lnga'i rgyan gyis mdzes//nyon mongs lnga'i bag chags sbyangs//khams gsum 'khor ba'i lam sgo gcad//rmad
59a2('a): 'byung rol pa ci yang snang //kun bzang dang du thams cad 'dus//mtshan ma bzangs ngan thams cad kun//ci yang snang zhing ngo bo stong //stong pa'i ngang las rtag gnas na//dus gsum rgyal ba'i dgongs pa yin//rdo rje mkha' 'gro'i rgyud
59a3('a): las/lta ba rgya mtsho'i le'u ste/bzhi pa'o///'phra men rgya mtshos yang gsol pa spyod pa'i don chen ji ltar lags//rgyal po bde bas bka' stsal pa//spyod mchog dam pa 'di ltar ste//rgyal po'i tshogs kyi
59a4('a): du na 'byor pa'i yo bad ldan bas nas//sna tshogs yi ges gzhal yas rlab//drag po'i sa bon gyis//bdud rtsi chen po'i longs spong ldan//g.yas pa ta ka'e'i 'basang bau ya ni//ga yon pa ta ro bcad bas pas//hril sgo 'bar
59a5('a): khas rol pos sta ba//'ba' ta bdud rtsi 'bar ba yang //phar stong tshur len 'bar bar byas//lam dgu thig le thig pa yang //bstan pa'i tshul ni gong ma bzhin/ta las ta'i rdzas rnams dang //bdud rtsi chen po'i thabs su rol/
59a6('a): 'byung 'jug kun la ri kris bsrung //yab las rgyal ba rigs lnga bsrung //yum las rgyal mo lnga yi mdzes//rdo rje yi ge rdzogs pas brgyan//b+ha gar dbyings phyug rgyal mos te//thabs kyi spyan ni rdeg par byas//shes rab spyan/
59a7('a): ni gdang byos te//na sa rtse mor hUM gzugs to//rta mchog g.yes chos g.yon du dri'o//'ong po'i sgra la hUM zhes brjod//'gro ba'i sgra la phaT du skrag so//de nas mkha' 'gro mchod byas te//rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma tshogs kun gyi/
59b1('a): sangs rgyas rnal 'byor kun gyi mes gcig ma//bde chen yum gyi dbyings nas gshegs su gsol//mkha' bzhi dbyings las skyed pa'i sras mo lnga//'byung ba lnga dang dus bzhi cha snyoms ma/mi 'gyur g.yu drung skyed pa'i sa
59b2('a): gnyis med sku//rgyal mo lnga la sogs pa gshegasu gsol//kun bzhi sems las skyed pa khro mo tshogs//phyir mi 'ong ba'i spun drug cu bzhi yi//rus men spyan drung tshegs rnams grwa shegasu gsol//phyi nang yul la skyed pa'i lnga
59b3('a): bcu lnga//mtshan med na mkha' lta bu'i rang bzhi las//nyi zla gza' skar mang po shar ba ltar//gnas 'dir 'khor bcas ma lus gshegs su gsol//sa ma yAM du na stam//rdo rje zhing chen stsol ba'i gdan stangasu//nyi zla pad+ma'i 'od kyi
59b4('a): dkyil 'khor bcas//dbyings kyi spyan drug sgom pa ma mo'i tshogs//lnga bcu rtsa lnga tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor tshogs//mi g.yo ma 'gul brtan par bzhugs su gsol//spod pa'i ngo bo 'di lta ste//u ma tshang na sa nyal ba dang //bcu drug
59b5('a): tshang nas sdod pa dang //phugs nas sram ltar 'phen pa dang //mkha' la rlung ltar 'phyo ba dang //rngam pa'i drag shul byed pa dang //na mkha'i skas la 'dzeg pa dang //klag tshon gzan la dbab pa dang //'phyar ba 'phyang thag chad pa dang /
59b6('a): glang chen rol du zhugs pa dang //mgal med klad la skor ba dang //'di rnams dmigs kyis sgral ba'o//pha dang khu bo mes po dang //lha dang bram ze mu stegs dang //bran dang g.yog po bdud dang ni//gshin rje dregs pa sgral ba'o//nyon med
59b7('a): yid dang rnams shes yid//kun gzhi sems dang 'dod chags sems//nga rgyal dang ni khong khri dang //ser sna dag dang rgyu dag dang //le le dang ni blag dag rnams//'di rnams dmigs kyis sprul ba'o//nyon mongs gzugs phung bshig
60a1('a): pa'i phyir//g.yas pa spu gri 'bar ba la//g.yon pa gyad kyis brgyangs nas su//tra tha'i sngags kyis sgo phye nas/ha la bsdus la brtse la bstim//'byung ba gsum gyis sbyang byas nas nas//rdo rje lcags kyis sbrus byas nas//
60a2('a): kun du bzang mo mkha' la gzhag/gser dang dngul du 'dres pa bzhin//bsdus nas rgyas pa'i phyag rgya skyed//zla rlung sbyor ba'i thabs kyis 'phang //rgyal ba'i rigs kyi sras su bskyed//dge sngogau las kyi shan phye nas//sdig pa byang nas ye shes
60a3('a): 'bar/sbyor ba chen po'i dam tshig ni//ma dang bsru mo phyi mo dang //lha mo klu me rogs ngan mo//rdol ba mo dang phyal ba dang /rma ba mdog sdug thabs kyis rlab//da 'od ri ka dbye ba dang //sgra'i thal mo sbyar ba dang //mkha' la rlung gis skyod pa dang
60a4('a): lcags sgrog phyag rgya gdams pa dang //sreg pa'i me yis dbab pa dang //ye shes rlung gis skrag pa dang //las kyi rlung gis bzlog pa dang //dgyes pa'i sa bon spro ba ste//'di rnams sbyor ba'i thobs su bstan//de las sa dgu don gyis rdzos
60a5('a): ye shes dgu dang yang dag ldan//rdo rje mkha' 'gro'i rgyud las//tshogs dang bstan 'gan gyi spyod pa bstan pa'i le'u ste/lnga pa'o///'ag men rgya mtshos yang gsol pa//gcan 'phang chu'i rang bzhin 'di//rim pa gang
60a6('a): nga gang lags pa//ngo mtshar lha la bdag nyan 'tshal//yang dag rdzogs kyis bka' stsalau pa//rgyal po bka' rtags mi bcag pa//ji ltar sgo ba'i las bya zhing //lus kyi bsrang ba thub pa dang /ngag gi skyon sgra yon tan brjod//sems
60a7('a): kyi gya gyu med pa ni//rgyal po bka' yi dam tshig go//rdo rje rig pa 'dzin rnams la//lus ngag yid kyi rtse sdom zhing //skyongs med pa'i dad pa yis//rtse gcig yid kyis mos pa byo//'di ni sku'i sa ma yAM//rig pa'i phyag rgya gtan bsgom/
60b1('a): shing tu 'od kyi rgyan gyi lha gsal ba//sku gsung thugs la gtan gnas te//nyi ma'i snying po gtan gsal ba//'di ni tsat+te'i sa ma yA//thig le 'bar ba'i sgra byung zhing //bskyed rdzogs sa bon rgyun mi bcad//spros pas lha mnyes ngan song skyong //
60b2('a): snang srid sngags kyi 'brang bar gsal//'di ni gsung gi sa ma yA//'di yi bka' gzhung ma thub par//phyin ci log du spyad par rin//'di ru mi bde sna tshogs 'byung //'bras bu rdo rje dmyal bar ltang //nyams pa'i rgyu ni 'di lta ste//dad pa chu bzhin/
60b3('a): ser sna che//khro btum che zhing bag med pa//ga yu sgyu che la phrag dog pa//'di ni nyams pa'i rgyu yin no//rtogs pa'i dam tshig gzhung thub na//'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten pa'i//mkha' 'gro rang bzhin nye ba la//bar tshad rkyen rnams mi
60b4('a): 'gyur zhing //dgra bgegs kun la bden pa 'jug/tshe ring ba dang rgyud 'phel 'gyur//rgyal po lta bur kun gyi bkur//ngan song gnas rnams stongs par 'gyur//nyams na bskang thabs 'di lta ste//sku'i dam tshig gzhung nyams na//
60b5('a): mkha' 'gro rnams kyis 'tshe 'gyur bas//lung ldan de la so ru gzhug/thugs kyi dam tshig nyams gyur na//rgyal ba'i yi dam spobs pa dbab//gsung gi dam tshig nyams gyur na//rang sngags phreng ba lab du bzlas/bskang gso'i cho ga
60b6('a): gzhan yang bya//rgyud kyi rgyal po rdo rje mkha' 'gro'i rgyud las//sa ma A dam tshig gi le'u ste/drug pa'o///'ag men rgya mtshos yang gsol pa//rgyal po bka' rtags ji ltar legs//ngo mtshar che ba bdag nyan 'tsha
60b7('a): mngon rdzogs yab kyis bka' stsal pa//rgyal po bka' rtags chen po ni//ngas bstan pa la sems thud cig/phyi dang nad dang gsang ba 'di//rab 'bring gsum gyi snod la skur/zhing gi ru mtshon 'bar ba 'di//rgyal sras lag du skur
61a1('a): @byas pas//srid pa'i mkha' 'gro dbang du 'gyur//dpung bzhi tshogs las rgyal bar byed//ka du rag sa ma yA s+t+waM//hU~M ral gri 'od chags 'bar ba 'dis//rgyal sras lag du bskur byas pas//nyon mongs 'khor ba'i dra ba//
61a2('a): cod srin po dregs pa'i khog pa dral//rag sha la kha da ga na hUM//dam nag 'bar ba'i ru mtshon 'dis//rgyal sras cod pani 'phya ba'i dbang //thabs dang shes rab dbang bskur bas//dus gsum gnas su sprul ba'i dbang //ha ha na nan phaT hUM//zod
61a3('a): tshar 'bar ba'i dbu rgyun can//rgyal mo lnga'i pho brang ste//nyon mongs lnga'i bag chags sbyangs//ye shes lnga'i 'od du sha ra//dz+nyA na ta ta hUM//nang gi don la 'di lta ste//rak+ta'i thig le 'bar ba 'dis//mkha' 'gro rig pa gsal bas te//che mchog nu ma'i
61a4('a): 'od du 'bar//lha mo rigs kyi bzhugs gnas so//mo ha la hUM//rak+ta'i thig le rgya gong du//thal ba'i tshem bu kha dog kha//rigs 'dzin rnam gsum pho brang nas//dug gsum rtsad nas bcod pa'i rtags//sku gsung thugs kyi don mtshon pa//pra tsa la
61a5('a): hUM thal ba'i rtags kyi rgya gong du//zo rig 'bar ba'i thig le lnga//rgyal ba'i rigs kyi pho brang ste//dug lnga gnas su sbyangs nas ni//ye shes lnga yis rgya yis gdab//pany+tsa dz+nyA na hUM/dam rdzas lnga'i thig le laM//rgyal mo lnga'i snying pos sbyangs//oM/
61a6('a): ldan dkar ba'i thig le yis//rnam snang dpal gyi ye shes don thob shog/hUM ldan 'thing la 'bar ba'i rang bzhin gyis//bkrag gsal snyan gyi ye shes don thob shog/swa ldan ser la 'bar ba'i thig le yis//'od gsal 'bar ba'i ye shes don
61a7('a): thob shog/oM ldan dmar la da' ba'i ngo bo 'di//gnyen gsal 'bar ba'i ye shes don thob shog/ha ldan ljang la sdom pa'i bdag nyid kyis/yang gsal g.yos kyi ye shes don thob shog/gsang ba'i don la 'di lta ste//rdo rje rigs kyi mtshan
61b1('a): ldan ma//khrus kyi dri ldan lha mo slab//seng ge stag gi g.yas phab nas//glang po 'gying bag g.yon phab nas//gyi lings rdzu 'phrul chen po dang //gcan zan tha ma rtul shugs dang //rgyu ba'i rlung ni na sar bzung //las kyi rlung ni rgya gram bzung //khyab byad
61b2('a): rlung ni rgya gram bzung //khyab byed rlung ni gti mug ste//rlung gsum bzung bas brtul shug mchog/re re la yang bzhi bzhis te/ye shes bcu drug ma ta ru//lhan cig skyes pa'i bde dbang bskur//oM rdo rje rigs kyi yum mchog ma//kha da mchog
61b3('a): ye shes skyed byed ma//g.yo ba lnga la ye shes lnga//'dzin bzhi bcu drug rtogs pa'i thabs//um mchog ma yis snang bar mdzod//rang rig gsal ba'i ye shes 'od//skad cig rtogs pa'i thabs mchog 'di//skye med yum dang rgyal po yang//
61b4('a): bdag la smon pa'i don gsal ba//rgyal ba'i sku dang bka' drin cig/ho rig pa'i rgyal po sras mdzes des//lus kyi skur pa phun sum tshogs//ngag gi skyon sla yon tan brjod//sems kyis rtags du 'dul btsas la//yum dang rgyal po thun
61b5('a): mong bskur//srog lus man cod lus kyi rnams//rgyal mo mchod pas sangs rgyas mnyes//de ltar thob pa'i yon tan ni//lus la 'tshe ba mi 'byung zhing //skye bo kun gyis bskur ba dang //mkha' 'gro ma rnams nye ba dang //rgya byin tshe dang mnyam pa
61b6('a): ngos he ru hU~M sras 'khor rnams mnyes//rgyal ba nyid dang dgongs pa mnyam//de ltar ma tho ba gang zag de//bsgrubs pa'i las la 'bras med 'gyur//'jig rten 'di la las log pas//phyi mar ngan 'gro'i sdug bsngal 'byung //rdo rje mkha'agro'i
61b7('a): rgyud las/dbang byin pa'i le'u ste/bdun pa'o///'phra min rgya mtshos yang gsol pa//dkyi mi 'khor rim pa gang legs pa/mngon rdzogs dpal la bdag nyan 'tshal//rgyal sras de bas bka' stsal pa//bdag nyid byang chu
62a1('a): thig le la//skye 'gag sang bar 'bas te//mi dmigs ma skyes dbyings nyid las//chos rnams 'byung ba'i yang bzhin tu//rtswa sprid mi 'gags rig pa rtsa lag ma 'gags rig pa rang shang ba//na mkha ba sgyung rje ma chag
62a2('a): su sbyor ba'i snying rje ste//dbyings dang ye shes rang bzhin las//gnyis med rig pa thugs kyi mchog/rig pa gsal ba ye shes brgyan//mthing ka hUM ni thugs kyi gter//e A ga ni A/du kar ma rag sha la raM dz+wa la maN+Dal/ma ti ga ra ma/kau nir
62a3('a): tri l-i/ra ma gri sha bi shu d+d+he/pad+ma tsakra ti ra ba ma//mthing ka hUM gi 'thur rlung la//drag po na mkha'i dkyil 'khor snang //skyod byed 'brar ba'i dbang gi dkyil 'khor 'byung //rak+ta dmar smug zhi ba'i dkyil 'khor chags//sha chen dpungs pas rgyal ba'i dkyil
62a4('a): 'khor la//keng rus khrel khrol rus pa'i ri rab gtsigs//sna tshogs b+h+rUM ma las pho brang gzhal yas shar//'khor lo rtsibos la yum gyi mkha' bzhis brgyan//rtsibs chen bzhi las bzhi'i dkyil 'khor shar//dbus kyi sharba nur nyi zla 'bar ba la//
62a5('a): phyi mkhyam b+ha ka bzhi'i bar 'khyams la//dus bzhi 'byung ba bzhi'i gdan du gsal//bsum pa pad+ma 'dab brgyad pa ru skad//bzhi pa 'dab ma pad+ma rtsibasu ldan//gru chad bzhi la snum bu khyams gsum ldan//nang gi brtsig pa rin chen rnam lnga la//
62a6('a): brtsig pazod pa'i mkhar du gtams//lha brgyad gdung la klu brgyad pham du zer//gza' brgyad kha bang rgyu skar dral bu byas//rgyal chen rigs pani sbrul nag rgyan gyis brgyan//zhi'i gi rgyal mtshan sgo bzhir rgyan//nyi zla pad+ma'i 'od kyi gdan du gsal/
62a7('a): dbus su 'dra sa ma bsnol ba las//lha dbang po rgya mi cha nyi ma'i gdon//rtsibs pa ni'i steng du bdud ze zhi rgyal mo gdan/dus pazhi'i rgyal mo bzhi la sgrar lag ris gdan//phyogs sbyang brgyad ni gyes sde khros pa'i gdang //blam mo thams
62b1('a): cangan ma rlam zhing gi gang ma/dbang mo rtsa brgyad dang gyi blon bstan mo//d+hu chen dbus kyi gdan la gam pa las//myong gros te drag kro d+hA tha ris ma ya thA/bcom ldar che mchog te zag/kun du bzang ma ra ral pa las//dgyes pa'i ra/
62b2('a): ba garba du ba zhes su gsol//skye med kun du bzang mo la//snang srid rdzas su dbul du gsol//ra mkha' la klong chen dbyings nas bzhengs//mkha' bzhi dbus kyi dkyil 'khor la//dgyes pa gar gyi thig le spro//'khor lo 'dra/
62b3('a): rim pa ni//rim pa bzhi'i 'phar ba'i dbus//thig le spro bas yig 'bru shar//bskyed pa'i sngags kyis phyi ru gdon//bdag gi gser gyi nyi ma las//sa bon las kyi skur bskyed pa//badz+ra kro d+hi shwa ri ba ga pad+ma rgya/hUM/bdag gi 'od
62b4('a): chen kha dog las//sa bon las ni sku bskyed pa//badz+ra shan+ti pad+ma ga ru ti b+h+yo//bdag gi 'byung ba lnga po las//sa bon las ni skur bskyed pa//'gi sa lung hUM ta dza la par ram hUM ha//shi sha san+ti ha ka bi lang he ha//bdag gi dngas ma
62b5('a): bzhi po las//sa bon las ni skur bskyeng pa//mu tra~M ni/a shin+ta ni/kha gdun tri ne/kha mur dan+ti ni//bdag gi dngas ma bzhi pa las/sa bon las ni sku bskyed pa/raM dz+wa la nI/paM ting ka la/u tsit+ti \u0f7f/yang gaN+Da laM//bdag gi da
62b6('a): /ma brgyad po la//sa bon las ni skur skyed pa//oM sur+ya bi ti ye/pad+ma bi ta e to ye//kha ro byi ta a ti ye/sring 'gro byi ra a tI ye/oM badz+ra ko kau/badz+ri ti ka~M badz+ra ta ko/badz+ra ni la badz+ra ni la/badz+ra lan+ta badz+ra lan+ta/bdag gi gsang ba
62b7('a): bde chen las//sa bon las ni skur bskyed pa//za gram sa mu ram shwar za gram mur ram shwar badz+ra kha rag snying kha ra ga/bdag gi za sa ma'i sbu gu las//sa bon las ni skur bskyed pa//pra ban ka rdi b+h+yo//pra kwing wang gi ti b+h+yo//bdag gi
63a1('a): srid pa mdor sdus las//sa bon las ni skur bskyed ga tAg ragang shaM kha muny+tsa ma dza ga /traka rAg+na mu tra ngan//bdag gi dpag bsam shing bu las//sa bon las ni skur bskyed pa//kha mun pa tra ba tra rag sha/bran pa na ka ri
63a2('a): b+h+yo bdag gi dbang po dor 'dus gi/sa bon las ni skur brgyud pa/d+hI/rig pa rang dang do//ni la chu mi ta/kha do kau ta ba la/tsa ba la/pad+mA/tsug sha/ma ku ra k+Shi/dar bam dang sim/a a me'o/nu ba ru lu nu bo ru mu d+h+yaM/rdzag
63a3('a): ga sha rag sha/g.yas g.yon thabs dang shes rab las//sa bon las ni sbyar bskyed pa/ma tI dz+yA//byi to ts+tsha//puSh+Ti b+h+yo//mu ti b+h+yo/badz+dzi shitsI b+h+yo//dun+taM byaM//har+t+ti b+h+yo/grub ting b+h+yo//a pa ra la ta b+h+yo//da la du na b+h+yAM//'di na ma ma ya b+h+yo
63a4('a): bdag gi sring pa dbang phyug las/ma 'an las ni snang babyed // b+h+yoSh+Tha/ b+hU /hU~Mb+h+ya rdo rje //ran mo kha' gyu rgyu las//dkrol 'khor bskyang bar le'u hU~M/brgyad par/
63a5('a): hUM snang snyed thams cad yum gyi b+ha ga'i klong dus gsum bde gshegs 'byung ba lnga la rdzogs//'byung ba lnga nyid yum gyi b+ha gas rgyun//'das dang ma b+h+yon b+ha ga nyid kyis brgyan//bad chen yum gyi dbyings nas gshegasu gsol//mkha'
63a6('a): bani dbyings nas bskyed pa'i sras mo lnga//'byung ba lnga'i rgyal mo gshegasu gsol//dngas ma yum las bskyed pa'i mkha' 'gro'i tshogs//rje mo bcu bzhi tshogs rnams gshegasu gsol//snyigs ma rtsa las bskyed pa'i ma mo'i tshogs//rdza
63a7('a): bcu drug gi tshog so bcas gsheg su gsol//'byung po bzhi dang dus bzhi ma snyoms ma//dus pani rgyal po 'khor bcas gshegs su gsol//d+hU snang srong thamos cad kha lo rlobs zin po//gru rang sgo bzhi b+ha ga'i tshul du rgyar/
63b1('a): dbus ni mi 'gyur chos kyi bum pa la//'dab brgyad dbyings las 'khor lo rtsibs bcu bcas//bcig pa gnyis las snam bu sgo'i brgyan//shar du rta khrag lho ru mi khrag dang //nub du mda' sbyar//byang du bdud rtsi gur gum bdud rtsi sogs//rang bzhin
63b2('a): mtshan mo dus ni 'di nyid du//mda' bzhi rgyal mtshan gnam du yer re re//khrag dang zhag gi 'dab chu zu lu lu//ye shes bzhi'i me long 'od gsal lam sa la ma//ral gri rgyal chen dmag dpon chen po bzhi//'gro ba'i don brtan par gzhu
63b3('a): /su gsol//bdud rtsi phyag gi bum pa'i rgyu dag gi/hUM gi bstims shing rgyu sbyangs ha lnga 'bar//thugs las bzhi 'phros bstan par gzhugs/srid pa'i ri la 'gro ba'i mkha'agro//sra gnyis lu gu rgyud byas hUM gnyis lcags kyu'i tshul du rten//
63b4('a): oM shrI \u0f7fhe ru ka yo gai dzaM dza/sur+ya maN+Dal bi bam/dam tshig gtso 'khor ldon pa'i//sprul pa re re gdan du dgyel//mkha'i steng khung dbur gnas pa//na mkha'i mtha' la ral gri phab/tra ta phaT/saM sa rang gi sgo dbye nas/hUM gyi sngags kyis
63b5('a): leb rgan drangs//che mchog le ba rgan 'bar bar smin//oM A mri ta u tsat+ti a hUM a/shes rab snying rje dus cig 'bar//nub pa'i gdan nas bteg byas te//pho brang steng du zung te bzhengs//zhal sbyor tshul du gnas pa'o//gnyis sbyor ga
63b6('a): la zur bstan pa'o//rdo rje mkha' 'gro'i rgyud las//spyan drangs zhing dbang bskur ba'i le'u ste/dgu pa'o///hUM bcom ldan che mchog he ru ka/mthing nag 'jigs pa'i cha lugs can/dkar nag 'bar ba'i zhal
63b7('a): mngar ba//g.yas kyi dang po rdo rje la//g.yon pa du tshar ga'u 'dzin//g.yos kyi 'bad ma ma yong gzhi la/g.yon pa rin chen sring dbang bsgyur//g.yas kyi tha ma rtse gsum la/g.yon gyi tha ma tshe 'dzin cing //mi bzang gtor
64a1('a): gyi mchod par bzhes//kun du bzang mo bde ba'i yum//bzhi zur zhabs brgyad phag drug 'bar//g.yas kyi dang po sring rtse 'dzin//g.yon gyi dang po rgya mtsho srog//ga yas kyi bar ma yab la 'khyud//g.yon//
64a2('a): gyi ba mas dung mar stog//g.yas kyi tha ma ru mtshon pa'o//g.yon gyi tha ma ru me dpung rdeg/me bzang gtir kyi mchod par bzhes//lbos mo mali'u chung rtse gnan nas//gung ma rtse sprad bzhi bsgreng ba//rgyal po lnga'i phyag rgya yini//g.yas
64a3('a): pa 'dzub mo sgreng ba la//g.yon pa chang ba 'khyud pa ni//dus bzhi rgyal mo phyag rgya yin//g.yas pa khu tshur snying khar bstan//g.yon pa mtshon gyi byeng pa yin//'phra men rgya mtsho nyid kyi rgya//'theb 'dzub rtse mo so sor sprang //spyan gyi thad kar
64a4('a): 'dzin pa ni//de ni rdo rje nyi ma'i rgya//'theb mo gnyis dang 'dzub mo gnyis sgong //gsum po phan tshun thags su btags//da ni sbas pa ri ma bzhi'i rgya//gung mo sA'i lag so sor dgug/mthe bong gnyis kyis so sor gnan//de ni khros pa'i rgyal ma/
64a5('a): rgya 'u chu mthe bong mthe bong rtse la mnan//lhag ma gsum po 'di ltar byas//thugs kyi dbye lnga rgya chen yin//phan tshur thabs su btags pa la//phyi ru 'gyed nas 'od 'phros pas//sman mo rnams kyi phyag rgya yin//nyi shu rtsa brgyad pa
64a6('a): phyag rgya ni//gzhan dang 'dra ste 'dir ma bshad//me tog rgyan gyi mchod pa chen po 'dri//snang srong mdzes pa'i mchod pa chen po bzhes//me bdud spos kyi dung ba chen po 'di//tshim pa'i mchod pa chen po bzhengs su gsol//bdud rtsi chen
64a7('a): po'i ngam skong mchod par bzhes//'dod chags khrag gi mchod pa dam par bzhes//pad+ma 'byung rgyan gyi gtor ma'i mchod par bzhengs/zhe sdang ting 'dzin ram gyi mchod par bzhes//'dod chags sbyor byed mchod pa
64b1('a): la la dril che sgra sgrogs khra la la//la la rol pa'i rta bro tsham se chems//rol mo ye shes mchod pa chen po bzhes//gnam lcags dur mo me'i mchod pa bzhes//gnam la 'phur dang chu la 'phyo ba dang //sa la skyes pa'i mdangs kyi mchod
64b2('a): pa ba zhes//ho dgongs su gsol//ma mo mkha' 'gro phyi nang gi phyogs su bsgyur ba dang //'brug gi sgra sgrogs pa dang //klog gi lce 'byin pa dang //gnam lcags kyi rkang rdeg pa dang //rgyu 'byung ba lnga kha lo bsgyur ba'i rgyal mo lnga dang /las/
64b3('a): mdzad srid pa zlos pa'i dbang mo tsha bzhi//dngas ma'i dbyings las shar ba'i'i mtshan bzhi dang //rig pargyan gyi mchen bzhi dang //dman mor grags pa thams cad dang //mkhyen pa dang byin gyis rlab tu gsol//de nas rig pa don
64b4('a): bzla //a~M hriHhe ru ka/badz+ra bo d+he tsat+ta sa ma yA hUM ho//kro b+hi shwa ri pad+ma ru lu ru lu b+h+yo//thugs la ljags kyi rtse la 'phro//mkha' la 'du zhing dbyings su spu/dkyil 'khor gnyis sdebs zhal skor bzlas//rang rang rtsa ba'i snying po spro//rdo rje mkha
64b5('a): 'gro'i rgyud las//mchod pa rim par bstan pa'i ste/bcu pa'o///hUM thugs kyi kun mkhyer sku'i kun du khyab//gsung gi sgra sgrogs yon tan cir yang snang //phrin las rgya mtsho mdzad la phyag 'tsha lo//ci mi
64b6('a): ba'i dbun drang bcu gsum po//rus mon dpur drug tshogs la phyag 'tshal lo//rig pa'i dbyings las shar ba'i dam can tshegs//thugs rje rtse cig yid kyis dgongs nas gshegs//srid pa kun gyi las rnams grub po mo//srid pa'i
64b7('a): sman mo'i tshogs laM phyag 'tshalo//de nas gdani rnams dkyil nos su//grub pa'i las rnams byed par bsgo//hUM bcom ldan che mchog he ru ka/mthing nag 'bar ba'i cha lugs la//dur khrod 'bar bas sku la brgyan//phyag drug phyag chas
65a1('a): skye shi sgrol//bskal pa me'i gzi mdangs can//yum dang gnyis su med par rol//zhugs rje dbyings nas rol pa mdzad//thugs rje mi 'gyur dbyings nas sku sprul pa//mkha' drug rgyal mo snga rdos spyod las gyis shig/
65a2('a): ming migs sgyu med dbyings nas sku sprul pa//'phra men rgya mtsho la sogs las gyis shig/snying rje chen po'i 'od las sku sprul ma//nyi ma thod phreng la sogs las kyis shig/thugs kyi sprul pas khams gsum rdul du rmugs//khros pa'i
65a3('a): dbang chen la sogs pas gyis shig/skye med mi 'gyur dbyings nas don mdzad ma//sbas pa'i ma chen la sogs las gyis shig/rig pa'i 'od las shar ba'i bzhad sgra can//thugs kyi dgyes sde la sogs las gyis shig/snang srid tham
65a4('a): cad skye 'gro'i rnam sprul ma//cha byed cha snyoms la sogs las gyis shig/dpag bsam shing bu khyer nas sprul pa'i lha//e ka tsa ti la sogs las gyis shig/'thung rlung drag pos snang srid b+h+yo zlog byed//na mkha' rgyal mo la sogs las gyis
65a5('a): shig/'dod chags gnas su stobs mdzad byams snyoms ma//'dod chags nas mtshan la sogs las gyis shog/bdud kyi bu smad srid gsum b+h+yo bzlog mdzad//srin mo chen mo la sogs las gyis shig/thugs rje byams pas
65a6('a): yab la brtse ba ma//las kyi srin mo la sogs las gyis shig/rtul shug stobs kyi bdud kyi dpung 'joms ma//dmag dpon rgyal po la sogs las byis shig/rgyal ba'i bka' bzhin las rnams khas blangs pa//nyi shu rtsa brgyad dbang mo las
65a7('a): kyis shis/dam khri bud las/'phrul ba'i ga bya/ mu ga rnam 'dra su mas yongags sa/ pa gang dgas pa/ sa dag ra ras
65b1('a): ka' stsal pa//sems gtan pa gnas sun// la zla ban yang dag rdzun/yang sus skyen pa rdzogs byas nas/kun du zang mo ga //rgyal ma bzhi dang bur mang ga pa//ts+tshadz+dzadz+dzadz+dza/d+hAd+hAr+dz+dzak+Sha/Sha dz+wa la ni phaT/sa tings la
65b2('a): phaT/rapu tsan+ti ha phaT/yang gar+b+he lang phaT/te pang phaT/a par lan+ta phaT/da la du na phaT/dngas ma 'di dngas ma phaT/sa'i dus nyal chen mos te/shar phyogs khams nas gshegs su gsol//me'i 'od nas rnam sprul ma//lho
65b3('a): gas khams nas gshe gsangasu gsol//chu'i dngas las sprul pa mo//nub phyogs khams nas gshegs gsol//rlung gi byad las snyed pa mo//byang phyogs klong nas ga ga gisangasu gsol//lha mo bu mo 'dab bzang mo//
65b4('a): ga shin rje'i bu mo ral can//klu'i bu mo yid 'phrog ma//gnod sbyin bu mo gar mkhan ma//phyogs bzhi dag nas gshegs su gsol//thimo pa'i rgyal mo bye brag ni//rgya mtsho gzugs kyi khog pa ru//gser mig 'bar ba'i shan
65b5('a): bskang /rgya mtsho sgra'i khog pa ru//tsa ra ka'i ba'i mda' yis bska //na khro ta'i khog b+ha ru//sdong byed ma'i bcud kyis bstams//gnod sbyin 'bar ba'i rtse mo ru//gangs kyi zhun chu 'bar bas skang //'thun pa'i rdzas kyis ska mnyes
65b6('a): bya Ta spyi dpal chen po'i mchod byas nas//rang rang sngags kyi mchod par bya//lho'i 'bar bya'i e dkyil du//rten gyi 'khor lo dmigs po yis//sro rtso na'i thag pas gdags//gzog mo bye thur dge la stong //hUM dpal gyi pho nya chen
65b7('a): mo ni//ston zla gsum gyi ngos shed la//khri gdugs bdal ba'i shar phyogs nas//srid pa 'breng bu sha za ma//shar nas dam sri sha dang phrol//dgun zla gsum gyi ngos shes las//khri gdugs bdal ba'i shar phyogs nas/
66a1('a): srid pa'i 'phreng nyal drod 'phrog ma//lho nas dam sri drod dang phrol//dbyar zla gsum gyi ngos shed la//khri gdugs bdal ba'i nub phyogs nas//srid pa lta sbyor khrag 'dzag ma//nub nas dam sri khrag dang /
66a2('a): grol khri gdugs bdal ba'i bang phyogs nas//srid pa'i gan skyal dbugs 'phrog ma//byang nas dam sri dbugs dang phral//hUM gling bzhi mgyogs ma shar nas gshegs//mkha' lo khyung lding lho nas gshegs//dmar mo ga ya//

66a4('a): ngam gri brlag byed khrag gi lag cha//bud byed dregs pa'i rdo kha sgril gyi le'u ste/dzasuM gnyis pa'o///dben la gtan pa'i gnas su ni//maN+Dal 'khor yug ldan pa la//'khor lo rtsibs brgyad la/
66a5('a): ba la//rdo rje 'bar ba me 'od ldan//a ba yum bsnyen pa rdzogs byas nas//yum gyi mkha' dkyil b+ha ga la//rtsibs brgyad 'khor lo 'bar ba la//lha mo tshom bu spro bar bya//tsA pa la phaT/ma ring ni phA Ta/ma ring ni phyaT/dud pa
66a6('a): rI phaT/mA ri phaT/sid+d+ha phaT/gaN+Dal pa phaT/u ba te laT/nu ma ra~M phaT/phyogs 'tshams brgyad nas bskum gdon/grub pa'i mtshan rtags kyi stan drugaso ma//hUM salung stong ma'ispyo//sna tshogs sbyin
